      <div n="6" type="sthāna">


        <head xml:lang="en">[Uttaratantra 39-24-57 Kāyacikitsātantra]</head>

        <note xml:lang="en">The Nepalese MSS do not follow the sequence of the vulgate here. The
          Nepalese edition should follow the Nepalese MSS, i.e., vulgate 6.38 is followed by
          6.60</note>

        <div n="39" type="adhyāya">
          <head xml:lang="en">[Adhyāya 39, Kāyacikitsā 1 : draft edition based on MS K]</head>

          <note xml:lang="en">[This adhyāya is currently a rough version based on MSS NAK 5-333 and
            699, being edited (2023-10-04).]</note>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.1"><l>
            
            
             athāto jvarapratiṣedhaṃ adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||<anchor xml:id="SS.6.39.1-n1"/>
          </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.1]</trailer>
</lg>

          

          
          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.2"><l>
            
            
             atha khalu vatsasuśruta </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.2]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.3"><l>
            
            
             yenāmṛtam apāṃ madhyād uddhṛtaṃ pūrvvajanmani |
            <caesura/>
             yato 'maratvaṃ saṃprāptās tridaśās tridiveśvarāḥ ||<anchor xml:id="SS.6.39.3-n1"/>
          </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.3]</trailer>
</lg>

          

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.4"><l>
            
            
             śiṣyās taṃ devam āsīnaṃ papracchuḥ suśrutādayaḥ |
            <caesura/>
             vraṇasyopadravāḥ proktā vraṇinām apy ataḥ paraṃ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.4]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.5"><l>
            
            
             samāsato vyāsataś ca brūhi no bhiṣajām vara |
            <caesura/>
             upadraveṇa juṣṭasya vraṇaḥ kṛcchreṇa sidhyati || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.5]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.6"><l>
            
            
             upadravāś ca vraṇinaḥ kṛcchrasādhytamo mataḥ |
            <caesura/>
             prakṣīṇabalamāṃsasya śeṣadhātuparikṣayāt || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.6]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.7ab"><l>
            
            
             tasmād upadravān kṛtsnān vadasva vadatām vara || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.7ab]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.7cd"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.8"><l>
            
            
             teṣān tad vacanaṃ śrutvā prābravīd bhiṣajām varaḥ ||
            <caesura/>
             jvaram ādau pravakṣyāmi sa rogānīkarāṭ smṛtaḥ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.8]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.9"><l>
            
            
             rudrakopāgnisaṃbhūtaḥ sarvvabhūtapratāpanaḥ ||
            <caesura/>
             tais tair nnāmabhir anyeṣāṃ sattvānām parikīrttyate | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.9]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.10"><l>
            
            
             janmādau nidhane caiva bhavatīha na saṃśayaḥ ||
            <caesura/>
             ataḥ sarvavikārāṇāṃ jvaro rājā prakīrttitaḥ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.10]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.11"><l>
            
            
             ṛte devamanuṣyebhyo nānyo viṣahate tu taṃ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.11]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.12"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.13"><l>
            
            
             svedāvarodhaḥ santāpaḥ sarvvāṅgagrahaṇam tathā | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.13]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.14"><l>
            
            
             yugapad yatra roge tu jvara ity upadiśyate ||
            <caesura/>
             doṣaiḥ pṛthak samastaiś ca dvandvair āgantur eva ca | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.14]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.15"><l>
            
            
             anekakāraṇotpannaḥ smṛtas tv aṣṭavidho jvaraḥ ||
            <caesura/>
             doṣāḥ prakupitā gāḍham apathyāhārasevinaḥ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.15]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.16"><l>
            
            
             vyāpya deham aśeṣeṇa jvaram āpādayanti ha || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.16]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.17"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.18"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.19ab"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.19cd"><l>
            
            
             adhimithyā<pc> </pc>prayuktaiś ca snehādyaiḥ karmmabhir nnṛṇāṃ |<anchor xml:id="SS.6.39.19cd-n1"/>
          </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.19cd]</trailer>
</lg>

          

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.20"><l>
            
            
             vividhād abhighātāc ca rogotthānāt prapākataḥ ||
            <caesura/>
             śramāt kṣayād ajīrṇṇāc ca viṣāt sātmyartuparyayāt | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.20]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.21"><l>
            
            
             oṣadhīpuṣpagandhāc ca kokānnakṣatrapīḍayā ||
            <caesura/>
             abhiśāpābhicārābhyāṃ manobhūtābhiśaṅkayā | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.21]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.22"><l>
            
            
             strīṇām avaprajātānāṃ prajātānān tathāhitaiḥ ||
            <caesura/>
             stanyāvataṇe jvaro doṣaiḥ prakupyati | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.22]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.23"><l>
            
            
             tair vvegavadbhir bbahudhā samudbhrāntai rvvimārggagaiḥ ||
            <caesura/>
             vikṣipyamāṇo 'ntaragnirbhbhavatyāśu bahiś caraḥ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.23]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.24"><l>
            
            
             ruṇaddhi cāpy apān dhātuṃ yasmāt tasmāj jvarāturaḥ ||
            <caesura/>
             bhavaty atyuṣṇagātraś ca svidyatena ca sarvvaśaḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.24]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.25"><l>
            
            
             śramor atirvivarṇṇatvaṃ vairasyaṃ nayanaplavaḥ |
            <caesura/>
             icchādveṣau muhuś cāpi śītavātātapādiṣu || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.25]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.26"><l>
            
            
             jṛmbhāṅgamarddo gurutā romaharṣo 'rucis tamaḥ |
            <caesura/>
             apraharṣaś ca śīta ca bhavaty utpatsyati jvare || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.26]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.27"><l>
            
            
             sāmānyato viśeṣāt tu jṛmbhāty arthan samīraṇāt |
            <caesura/>
             pittān nayanarddāhaḥ kaphād annārucis tathā || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.27]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.28"><l>
            
            
             sarvvaliṅgasamavāyaḥ sarvvadoṣaprakopaje |
            <caesura/>
             rūpair anyatarābhyān tu saṃsṛṣṭe dvandvajaṃ viduḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.28]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.29"><l>
            
            
             vepathurvviṣamo vegaḥ kaṇṭhauṣṭhapariśoṣaṇaṃ |
            <caesura/>
             nidrānāśaḥ kṣayastaṃbho gātrāṇāṃ raukṣyam eva ca || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.29]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.30"><l>
            
            
             śiroruggātra rugvaktraṃ vairasyaṃ gāḍhaviṭkatā |
            <caesura/>
             śūlādhmānau jṛmbhaṇañ ca bhavaty anilaje jvare || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.30]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.31"><l>
            
            
             vegastīkṣṇomatisāraś ca mahādāho nidrālpaś ca tathā vamiḥ |
            <caesura/>
             kaṇṭhauṣṭham pākaḥ svedaś ca jāyate || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.31]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.32"><l>
            
            
             pralāpo vaktra kaṭukā mūrcchā dāho madastṛṣā |
            <caesura/>
             pītaviṇmūtranetratvam paittike bhrama eva ca || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.32]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.32a"><l>
            
            
             staimityaṃ stimito vega ālasyam ma dhurāsyatā |
            <caesura/>
             śuklamūtrapurīṣatvaṃ stambhastṛptirathāpi ca || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.32a]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.33"><l>
            
            
             gauravaṃ śītamutkledo romaharṣo 'tinidratā | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.33]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.34"><l>
            
            
             pratiśyāyo 'ruciḥ kāsaḥ kaphaje 'kṣṇoś ca śuklatā | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.34]</trailer>
</lg>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.38]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.39"><l>
            
            
             nātyuṣṇaśīto 'lpasaṃjñā bhrāntaprekṣī hatasvaraḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.39]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.40"><l>
            
            
             śvasannipatitaḥ śete pralāpopadravāyutaḥ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.40]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.41"><l>
            
            
             tam abhinyāsam ity āhur hataujasam athāpare ||
            <caesura/>
             sannipātajvaraḥ kṛcchram asādhyam apare viduḥ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.41]</trailer>
</lg>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.45]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.46"><l>
            
            
             punar ghorataro bhūtvā praśamaṃ yānti hanti vā ||
            <caesura/>
             dvidoṣocchrayaliṅgas tu dvandvajās trividhaḥ smṛtaḥ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.46]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.47"><l>

            <caesura/>
             kaṇṭhāsyaśoṣo vamathur llomaharṣo rucis tamaḥ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.47]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.48"><l>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.49"><l>

            <caesura/>
             tandrā mohaḥ pralāpaś ca śleṣmavātajvarākṛtiḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.49]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.50"><l>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.50]</trailer>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.63"><l>
            
            
             svaṃ kālam viṣamo 'bhyeti prasahyaṃ māruto jvaraḥ ||
            <caesura/>
             sa cāpi viṣamo dehan na kadācid vimuñcati | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.63]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.64"><l>
            
            
             yasmād gauravavaivarṇṇyaṃ kārśyebhyo na vimucyate ||
            <caesura/>
             vege tu samatikrānte gato yam iti lakṣyate | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.64]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.39.65"><l>
            
            
             dhātvantarastho līnatvān na saukṣmyād upalabhyate ||
            <caesura/>
             alpadoṣe tvana kṣīṇa kṣīṇetvana ipānalaḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.39.65]</trailer>

          <head xml:lang="en">[Adhyāya 42: draft edition based on MS K]</head>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.1"><l>
            
            
             athāto gulmarogapratiṣedhaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.42.1]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.2"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.3"><l>
            
            
             yathoktaiḥ kopanaidoṣaḥ kupitāḥ koṣṭhamāgatāḥ |
            <caesura/>
             janayanti nṛṇāṅgulmaṃ sa pañcavidha ucyate |</l>

             śoṣaṃ kuṣṭhodarāmīma samayec ca bhagandarāṃ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.42.40.add29]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.40.add30"><l>
            
            
             yonidoṣaharo vṛṣyo medhā valavapuḥ karaḥ |
            <caesura/>
             guḍakalyāṇasaṃkṣas tu mataś cāpi rasāyanaiḥ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.42.40.add30]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.40cd"><l>
            
            
             tile kṣurakapālāśa sārṣapaṃ yavanālajaṃ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.42.40cd]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.41"><l>
            
            
             bhasmāmūkajañ cāpi gojavikharahastināṃ |
            <caesura/>
             mūtreṇa mahiṣīṇāñ ca palikaiś cāpi cūrṇṇitaiḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.42.41]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.42"><l>
            
            
             kuṣṭhasaindhavayaṣṭhīkanāgarakrimihantibhiḥ |
            <caesura/>
             ajamodāc ca daśabhiḥ sāmudrāc ca palaiyutaṃ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.42.42]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.43"><l>
            
            
             ayas pātregnikalpena paktvā lehyam athoddharet |
            <caesura/>
             tasya mātrāṃ pibed dadhnā surayāṣā pi vā |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.42.43]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.44"><l>
            
            
             dhānyāmlenoṣṇatoyena kaulatthena rasena vā |
            <caesura/>
             gulmaṃ vātavikārāś ca kṣāroyaṃ hanty asaṃśayaṃ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.42.44]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.45"><l>
            
            
             svarjikākuṣṭahasahaiḥ kṣāraḥ ketakajo pi vā |
            <caesura/>
             pītatailena śamayed dulmaṃ pavanasaṃbhavaṃ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.42.45]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.46"><l>
            
            
             pītaṃ sukhāṃvunāvāpi svarjikākuṣṭhasaindhavaṃ ||
            <caesura/>
             vṛścīkamurūvūkaś ca varṣābhūvṛhatī dvayaṃ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.42.46]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.47"><l>
            
            
             citrakañ ca jaladroṇai pacet pādāvaśeṣitaṃ |
            <caesura/>
             māgadhācikumbhe nidhāpayet |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.42.47]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.48"><l>
            
            
             madhunaḥ prasthamāvāpya pathyācūrṇṇārdhasaṃyutaṃ |
            <caesura/>
             viśoṣitaṃ daśāhan tu jīrṇṇabhakaḥ piven naraḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.42.48]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.49"><l>
            
            
             ariṣthoyaṃ jayedgulmavipākaṃ ca dustaraṃ ||
            <caesura/>
             pāṭhānikumbharajanītrikaṭutriphal-agnikā | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.42.49]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.50ab"><l>
            
            
             lavaṇaṃ vṛkṣakavījāni cūrṇṇagomūtrasādhitaṃ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.42.50ab]</trailer>
</lg>
          
          <lg xml:id="SS.6.42.50cd"><l/></lg>

            <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.42.51]</trailer>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.1"><l>
            
            
             athāto hṛdrogapratiṣedhaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.1]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.2"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.3"><l>
            
            
             vegāghātoṣṇarūkṣān nairatimātropasevitaiḥ |
            <caesura/>
             viruddhādhyaśanājīrṇair rasātmyaiś cāpi bhojanaiḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.3]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.4"><l>
            
            
             dūṣayitvā rasāṃ doṣā viguṇā hṛdaṃ yaṃ gatāḥ |
            <caesura/>
             hṛdivādhāṃ prakuvyanti hṛdrogāṃ taṃ pracakṣate |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.4]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.5"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.6"><l>
            
            
             āyamyate mā||||||
            <caesura/>
             nirmathyate dīryate ca yodyatepi pāṭyatepi ca |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.6]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.7"><l>
            
            
             tṛṣṇoṣṇādāhacoṣāsyuḥ paittike hṛdayaklamaḥ |
            <caesura/>
             dhūmāyanaṃ ca mūrchā ca svedaḥ śoṣo mukhasya ca |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.7]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.8"><l>
            
            
             gauravaṃ kaphasaṃsrāvorūcistastambhognimārdavaṃ |
            <caesura/>
             mādhuryam api cā sasya valāsāpahate hṛdi ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.8]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.9"><l>
            
            
             utkleśaḥ ṣṭhīvanaṃ todaḥ śūlaṃ hṛllāsakovamī |
            <caesura/>
             aruciḥ śyāvanetratvaṃ śoṣaś ca krimijo bhavet |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.9]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.10"><l>
            
            
             bhramaklamau modaśoṣau jñeyās teṣām upadravāḥ |
            <caesura/>
             krimijo krimijātīnāṃ ślemikāṇāś ca yo matāḥ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.10]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.11"><l>
            
            
             vātopasṛṣṭe hṛdaye vāmayet snigdhamāturaṃ |
            <caesura/>
             dvipañcamūlīkvāthena sasnehalavaṇena tu ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.11]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.12"><l>
            
            
             pippaẏelāvacāhiṃguyavabhasmāni saindhavaṃ |
            <caesura/>
             saurvarcalaṃ tathā śunṭhī majomodaś ca cūrṇitaṃ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.12]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.13"><l>
            
            
             phaladhānyāmlakaulatthadadhimadyāsavādibhiḥ |
            <caesura/>
             pāyayet ca śuddhadehaś ca snehanānyatamena vā |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.13]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.14"><l>
            
            
             bhojayej jīrṇabhaiṣajyāṃ jaṃgalaisaghṛtai rasaiḥ |
            <caesura/>
             vātaghnasiddhaṃ tailañ ca vastiṃ dadyād vicakṣaṇāḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.14]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.15"><l>
            
            
             śrīpaṇimadhukakṣaudrasitāguḍajalair vamet |
            <caesura/>
             pittopasṛṣṭe hṛdayeś ca seveta madhuraśritaṃ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.15]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.16"><l>
            
            
             ghṛtaṃ kaṣāyaś coddiṣṭaṃ pittajvāravināśanaṃ
            <caesura/>
             tṛptasya ca rasaurmukhyair jaṃgalaiḥ saghṛtaibhiṣak |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.16]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.17"><l>
            
            
             sakṣaudraṃ vitared vastitailaṃ madhukasādhitaṃ |
            <caesura/>
             vacānimvakaṣāyābhyāṃ vāntaṃ hṛdi kaphotthite |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.17]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.18"><l>
            
            
             pāyayet cūrṇaṃ uddiṣṭaṃ vātake bhojayetc tam |
            <caesura/>
             valātailaṃ vidadyāc ca vastivastiviśāradaḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.18]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.19"><l>
            
            
             krimihṛdrogiṇāṃ snigdhaṃ bhojat piśitodanaṃ |
            <caesura/>
             daghnā ca phalalopetaṃ tryahaṃ paścād virecayet ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.19]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.20"><l>
            
            
             sugandhibhiḥ śalavaṇair yogaiḥ sājājiśarkaraiḥ |
            <caesura/>
             viḍaṃgagāḍhaidhānyāmlaṃ pāyayed hitam uttamaḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.20]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.43.21"><l>
            
            
             hṛdisthitāḥ patantyeva madhastāt krimayo nṛṇāṃ |
            <caesura/>
             yavānnaṃ vitarec cāsmaisaviḍaṃgamataḥ param iti || kāyaci || kṛ || ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.43.21]</trailer>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.1"><l>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.2"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.3"><l>
            
            
             vyāyāmamamlam lavaṇāni madyaṃ mṛdaṃ divāsvapnamatīvatīkṣṇaṃ |
            <caesura/>
             niṣevyamāṇasya vidūṣya raktaṃ kurvanti doṣās tvaci pāṇḍubhāvaṃ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.44.3]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.4"><l>
            
            
             pāṇḍvāmayoṣṭārdhavidhaḥ pradiṣṭaḥ pṛthaksamastaiyugapac ca dosaiḥ |
            <caesura/>
             sarveṣu caivaṣvihapāṇḍabhāvo yatodhikotaḥ khalu pāṇḍurogaḥ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.44.4]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.5"><l>
            
            
             tvaksphoṭanaṣṭhīvanagātra
            <caesura/>
             viṇmūtrapītatvamathāvipāko bhaviṣyatas tasya puraḥ sarāṇi |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.44.5]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.6"><l>
            
            
             malāpānnakipāṇḍurogaḥ |
            <caesura/>
             kumbhāhvayo lākarakolasākhyaḥ |
            <caesura/>
             vibhāsyate lakṣaṇamasya kṛtsnaṃ nivodhya vakṣyāmnunupūrvaśaste |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.44.6]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.7"><l>
            
            
             kṛṣnekṣaṇaṃ kṛṣṇasirāvanaddhaṃ tad varṇaviṇmūtranakhānanañ ca |
            <caesura/>
             vātena pāṇḍuṃ manujaṃ vyavasyed yuktatathānyais tad ūpadravaiś ca |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.44.7]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.8"><l>
            
            
             pītekṣaṇaṃ pītasirāvanaddhaṃ tad varṇaviṇmūtranakhānanañ ca |
            <caesura/>
             pittena pāṇḍum manujaṃ vyavasyed yuktatathānyais tad upadraiś ca |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.44.8]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.9"><l>

            <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.44.9]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.10"><l>
            
            
             abhaktarūgnair avagātra sādāḥ sarveṣu tandrī ca valakṣayañ ca |
            <caesura/>
             sarvātmake sarvamidaṃ vyavasyelliṅgāni vaks̤yāmy atha kāmalāyāḥ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.44.10]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.11"><l>
            
            
             yo hyāmayānte sahasānnamamlamadyād apathyāni ca tasya pittaṃ
            <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.44.11]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.12"><l>

            <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.44.12]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.13"><l>
            
            
             taṃ vātapittād haripītanīlaṃ halīmakettu pravadanti kecit |
            <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.44.13]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.14"><l>
            
            
             sophasthiruk kaṇṭhagatovalatvaṃ mūrcchā kaphe hṛdyavapīḍanañ ca |
            <caesura/>
             sādhye tu pāṇḍvāmayinaṃ samīkṣya snigdhaṃ ghṛtenordhamadhaś ca śuddhaṃ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.44.14]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.15"><l>
            
            
             sampādayet kṣaudraghṛtapragāḍhair harītakīcūrṇayutaiḥ prayogaiḥ |
            <caesura/>
             pibed ghṛtaṃ vā rajanādi pakvaṃyatraiphalaṃ tailvakam eva cāpi |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.44.15]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.16"><l>
            
            
             virecanādravyavipācitaṃ vā yogāś ca vairecanikāṃ ghṛtena |
            <caesura/>
             mūtre nikumbhārdhanaṃ vipācya pibed abhīkṣṇaṃ kuḍavārdhamātraṃ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.44.16]</trailer>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.17"><l>

            <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.44.17]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.18"><l>

            <caesura/>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.19"><l>

            <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.44.19]</trailer>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.20"><l>

            <caesura/>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.21"><l>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.31"><l>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.32"><l>

            <caesura/>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.33"><l>

            <caesura/>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.34"><l>

            <caesura/>

</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.35"><l>
            
            
             jvaraṃ tṛṣāṃ chardim athātisāraṃ mūrcchākṣayaṃśvāsamannalipsāṃ |
            <caesura/>
             tathā vipākasvarabhedasādāṃ jāyed yathāsvāṃ prasamīkṣadoṣāṃ ||</l>

</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.36"><l>
            
            
             anteṣu śūnaṃ parihīnamadhyaṃ mlānantathānteṣu ca madhyaśūnaṃ |
            <caesura/>
             gudeṣu śophastvathamuṣkaśūnaṃ prātamyamānañ ca visañjñakalpaṃ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.44.36]</trailer>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.44.37"><l>
            
            
            vivirjayet pāṇḍukinaṃ yaśorthī tathātisārajvarapīḍitañ ceti ||</l>

            <caesura/>
             tīkṣṇoṣṇakṣāralavaṇaiamlaiḥ kaṭvābhir eva ca |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.45.3x]</trailer>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.4x"><l>

            <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.45.4x]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.5"><l>

            <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.45.5]</trailer>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.8"><l>

            <caesura/>

<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.45.8]</trailer>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.9"><l>
            
            
             daurvalyaśvāsakāsajvaravathumadā pāṇḍutādāhamūrcchā
            <caesura/>
             bhukte cānne vidāhas tv adhṛtir api sadā hṛdya tulyā ca pīḍā |
            <caesura/>
             tṛṣṇākaṇṭhasya bhedaḥ śirasi ca pavanaṃ pūtiniḥ ṣṭhīvanañ ca
            <caesura/>
             dveṣo bhakte vipāko viratir api bhaved raktapittopasargāt ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.45.9]</trailer>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.10"><l>

            <caesura/>

            <caesura/>
             yat kṛṣṇaṃ yac ca nīlaṃ bhṛśam atikuṇapaṃ yatra coktā vikārāḥ
            <caesura/>
             tad varjyaṃ raktapittaṃ surapatidhanuṣā yac ca tulyaṃ vibhāti ||</l>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.11"><l>

            <caesura/>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.12"><l>

            <caesura/>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.16"><l>

            |</l>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.17"><l>

            <caesura/>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.18"><l>

            <caesura/>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.45.19"><l>
            
            
             madhūkaśobhāñjanakodirajaiḥ priyaṃgukānāṃ kusumaiś ca cūrṇanitaḥ |
            <caesura/>
             bhiṣag vidadhyāc caturaḥ samākṣikāṃ hitāya lehānasṛjāḥ praśāntaye |</l>

        <div n="48" type="adhyāya">
          <head xml:lang="en">[Adhyāya 48: draft edition based on MS K]</head>


          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.1"><l>
            
            
             athāta tṛṣṇāpratiṣedhaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.1]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.2"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.3"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.4"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.4a"><l>
            
            
             bhayabhramābhyāṃ rasasaṃkṣayād vā ūrdhvaṃ citam pittavivavanaiś ca |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.4a]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.4b"><l>
            
            
             pittaṃ savātaṃ kupitaṃ narāṇāṃ tāluprapannaṃ janayet pipāsāṃ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.4b]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.5"><l>
            
            
             srotaḥ svapāmvasvuṣudūṣiteṣu doṣais tṛṣāṃ sambhavatīha jantoḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.5]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.6"><l>
            
            
             tisraḥ smṛtā lohitajā caturthī kṣayāt tathānyām asamudbhavā ca |
            <caesura/>
             syāt saptamī bhaktasamudbhavā ca liṅgāni tāsāṃ śṛṇu sauṣadhāni ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.6]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.7"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.8"><l>
            
            
             śuṣkāmyatā mārutasambhavāyā todas tathā kaṇṭhaśirassu cāpi |
            <caesura/>
             sroto nirodhā virasaś ca vaktraṃ sītābhir adbhiś ca vivṛddhimeti |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.8]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.9"><l>
            
            
             mūrcchā pralāpo rucivaktraśoṣo raktekṣaṇatvaṃpratataś ca doṣa |
            <caesura/>
             sītābhikāṃkṣā mukhatiktatā ca pittātmikāyāṃ paridhūpaṇañ ca |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.9]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.9a"><l>
            
            
             bāṣpāvarodhaḥ kaphasambhṛtegnau tṛṣṇā balāsena bhavet tathā tu |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.9a]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.10"><l>
            
            
             nidrāgurutvaṃ madhurāsyatā ca bhayārditaḥ śuṣyati cātimātraṃ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.10]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.11"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.12"><l>
            
            
             kṣatasya rukchoṇitanirgamābhyāṃ tṛṣṇā caturthī kṣatajā bhavet sā |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.12]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.13"><l>
            
            
             raktakṣayādyā kṣayajā matāsau tayārditaḥ śuṣyati dahyate ca |
            <caesura/>
             abhyarthamākāṃkṣati cāpi toyaṃ tāṃ sannipātāditi kecidāhuḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.13]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.14"><l>
            
            
             raktakṣayoktāni ca lakṣaṇāni tasyāmaśeṣeṇa bhiṣagvyavasyet |
            <caesura/>
             tridoṣaliṅgāmasamudbhavā tu hṛcchūlaniṣṭhīvanasaṃprayuktā |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.14]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.15"><l>
            
            
             snigdhaṃ tathāmlaṃ lavaṇañ ca bhuktaṃ gurvannamevāpi tṛṣāṃ karoti |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.15]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.16"><l>
            
            
             tṛṣṇābhivṛddhāvudare ca pūrṇṇe taṃ vāmayet māgadhikodakena |
            <caesura/>
             vilekhanañcātra hitaṃ vadanti syāddāḍi māmrātakamātuluṃgaiḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.16]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.17"><l>
            
            
             tṛṣṇāprayogaiḥ prativāraṇīyā śītaiś ca samyagrasavīryajātaiḥ |
            <caesura/>
             gaṇḍūṣamamlair virase ca vaktre kuryāc chubhair āmalakasya cūrṇṇaiḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.17]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.18"><l>
            
            
             suvarṇṇarūpyādibhir agnitaptair loṣṭraiḥ kṛtair vā sikatāsu cāpi |
            <caesura/>
             jalaiḥ sukhoṣṇaiḥ śamayet tu tṛṣṇāṃ saśarkaraiḥ kṣaudrayutaṃ hitam vā ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.18]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.19"><l>
            
            
             pañcāṃgikāḥ pañcagaṇā ya uktās teṣv ambusiddhaṃ prathame gaṇe vā |
            <caesura/>
             pibaṃ sukhoṣṇaṃ manujo cireṇa tṛṣo vimucyeta hi vātajāyāḥ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.19]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.20"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.20a"><l>
            
            
             pittotthitāṃ pittaharair vipakvaṃ nihanti toyaṃ paya eva vāpi ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.20a]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.21"><l>
            
            
             bilvāḍhakīkanyasipañcamūlaṃ darbheṣu siddhaṃ kaphajaṃ nihanti |
            <caesura/>
             hitam bhavec chardanam eva cātra taptenanimbaprasavodakena |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.21]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.22"><l>
            
            
             sarvāsu tṛṣṇāsvathavāpi paittṃ kuryād vidhiṃ tena śamaṃvrajanti |
            <caesura/>
             prayāgatodumbarajorasas tu saśarkarāstvakkvathitodakam vā |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.22]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.23"><l>
            
            
             vargasya siddhasya ca śārivādeḥ peyaṃ jalaṃ syāt tu tṛṣābhibhūte |
            <caesura/>
             kaśeruśṛṃgāṭakapadmakaiś ca viśeṣu siddhaṃ tvathavā pibeta</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.23]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.24"><l>
            
            
             jalotpalośīrakucandanāni datvā pravāte niśi vāmayet tu
            <caesura/>
             taduttaman toyamudāragandhi sitāyutaṃ kṣaudrayutaṃ ca peyaṃ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.24]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.25"><l>
            
            
             drākṣāpragāḍhañ ca hitāya vaidyas tṛṣṇārditebhyo vitaren narebhyaḥ |
            <caesura/>
             saśārivādau tṛṇapañcamūlī tathotpalādau prathame gaṇe ca |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.25]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.26"><l>
            
            
             kuryāt kaṣāyāṇi yathaitaduktaṃ madhūkapuṣpādiṣu cāpareṣu |
            <caesura/>
             rājādanakṣīrikapītaneṣu ṣaṭpānakānyatra hitāni ca syuḥ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.26]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.27"><l>
            
            
             satuṇḍikerāṇyathavā pibet tu piṣṭāni kāryāni samudbhavāni |
            <caesura/>
             kṣatodbhavāṃ rugvinivāraṇena jāyedrasānāmasṛjaś ca pānaiḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.27]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.28"><l>
            
            
             kṣayotthitāṃ kṣīrajalaṃ nihanyān māṃsodakam vā madhurodakam vā |
            <caesura/>
             āmotthitāṃ bilvavacāyutānāṃ jāyet kaṣāyairatha dīpanānāṃ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.28]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.29"><l>
            
            
             gurvannadāmullikhanair jayec ca kṣayādṛte sarvakṛtāś ca tṛṣṇāḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.48.29]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.31"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.48.33"><l>

            <caesura/>
             saṃśodhanaṃ kṣīrarasāghṛtāni sarvāsu lehā madhurāṃ himāṃś ceti || ❈ ||</l>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.1"><l>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.2"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.3"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.6"><l>
            
            
             cchādayannānanaṃ vegair ardayann aṃgabhañjanaiḥ |
            <caesura/>
             nirucyate cchardir iti doṣo vaktrāt pradhāvitaḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.49.6]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.6a"><l>
            
            
             īrayañcchleṣmapittau tu udāno vyāpadaṃ gataḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.49.6a]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.7"><l>
            
            
             ūrdhvam āgacchati bhṛśaṃ viruddhāhārasevinaḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.49.7]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.8"><l>
            
            
             praseko hṛdayotkledobhaktasyānabhinandanaṃ |
            <caesura/>
             pūrvarūpāṃ mataṃ cchardyā yathāsvaṃ cāpi nirdiśet ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.49.8]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.9"><l>
            
            
             yaḥ phenilaṃ cchardayate lpamalpaṃ śūlārditobhyarditapārśvapṛṣṭhaḥ |
            <caesura/>
             śrāntaḥ saṇoṣaṃ bahuśaḥ salīnaṃ sāvātakopaprabhavātu cchardiḥ |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.49.9]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.10"><l>
            
            
             vyomvlaṃ bhṛśaṃ vā kaṭutiktavaktraḥpītaṃ saraktaṃ haritaṃ vamedvā |
            <caesura/>
             sadāhatodajvaravaktraśoṣaṃ sāpittakopaprabhavā matā tu |</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.49.10]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.11"><l>
            
            
             yo hṛṣṭaromā madhuraṃ prabhūtaṃ śuklaṃ himaṃ sāṃdrakaphānuviddhaṃ |
            <caesura/>
             abhaktaruggauravasādayuktaṃ vamedvamī sā kaphakopajā tu ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.49.11]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.12"><l>
            
            
             sarvāṇi liṅgāni bhavanti yasyāḥ sā sarvadoṣaprabhavā matā tu ||
            <caesura/>
             bībhatsajā dauhṛdajanmajātva sātmyaprakopāt krimijā ca yā hi |
            <caesura/>
             sā pañcamī tāñ ca vibhāvayīta doṣocchrayeṇaiva yathoktamādau ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.49.12]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.13"><l>
            
            
             śūlahṛllāsabahulā krimijā tu viśeṣataḥ |
            <caesura/>
             krimihṛdrogatulyena lakṣaṇena ca lakṣitā ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.49.13]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.14"><l>
            
            
             kṣīṇasyopadravavatīṃ sāsṛkprāyāṃ sacandrikāṃ |
            <caesura/>
             cchardiprasaktāṃ kuśalo nārabheta cikitsituṃ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.49.14]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.15"><l/></lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.49.16"><l>
            
            
             vamīṣu bahudoṣāsu cchardanaṃ hitamucyate ||

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.3"><l>
            
            
             doṣaiḥ pṛthak trividhasokasamucchrayāc ca bhaktāyaneṣu hṛdi cāvatate pragāḍhaṃ |
            <caesura/>
             nānne rucir bhavati taṃ bhiṣajo vikāraṃ bhaktopaghātam iha pañcavidhaṃ vadanti | | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.57.3]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.4"><l>
            
            
             hṛcchūlaśoṣavamathuḥ svarasaṃkṣayāc ca vātā sadāhamadatṛḍ vamane ca pittaṃ |
            <caesura/>
             pittāt kaphena hṛdaye gurutāvilepaḥ śleṣmaprasekavamathuḥ śiraso rujañ ca | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.57.4]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.5"><l>
            
            
             sarvātmakaṃ trividhaliṃgam atho vadanti ||
            <caesura/>
             śokopaghātajam athānilalakṣaṇais tu |
            <caesura/>
             vātātmake virasam āsyam arocake tu pittena tiktakaṭukaṃ madhuraṃ kaphena | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.57.5]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.6"><l>
            
            
             sarvair upetam upadhārayasannipāte | dainyaṃ bhṛśam bhavati śokasamudbhave tu |
            <caesura/>
             vāṃtovacādir anile vidhivat pibec ca snehoṣṇatoyamadirānyatamena cūrṇṇaṃ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.57.6]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.7"><l>
            
            
             kṛṣṇāviḍaṃgayavabhasmahareṇubhārgīrāsnailahiṃgulavaṇottamanāgarāṇāṃ |
            <caesura/>
             pitte guḍāmbumadhurair vamanaṃ praśastaṃ lehaś ca saindhavasitāmadhusarpiriṣṭaḥ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.57.7]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.8"><l>
            
            
             nimbāmbuccharditavataḥ kaphaje nupānaṃ rājadrumāmbu madhunā tu sadīpyakāḍhyaṃ |
            <caesura/>
             cūrṇṇaṃ yad uktam atha vā tilaje tad eva sarvaiś ca sarvakṛtam evam upakrameta | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.57.8]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.9"><l>
            
            
             drākṣāpaṭolaviḍavetrakarīranimbamūrvābhayākṣabadarāgalakendravṛkṣāḥ |
            <caesura/>
             bījaiḥ karañjanṛpavṛkṣabhavaiś ca piṣṭai lehaṃ pacet surabhimūtrayutaṃ yathāvat | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.57.9]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.10"><l>
            
            
             mustāvacākaṭukarohiṇinirdahanyas tulyātha vāpi rajanābhayasaṃprayuktā |
            <caesura/>
             mūtre vike dviradamūtrayutāḥ paced vā pāṭhāsamām ativiṣāṃ rajanāñ ca mukhyāṃ | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.57.10]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.11"><l>
            
            
             maṇḍūkim arkam amṛtāṃ raśalāṃgalākhyāṃ mūtre paceta mahiṣasya vidhāṇavidvān |
            <caesura/>
             etān na santi caturo bhyasatas tu lehān gulmāruciś ca śikhisādahṛdāmayāś ca | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.57.11]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.12"><l>
            
            
             sātmyāsvadeśacaritāṃ vividhāṃś ca bhakṣyāṃ pānāni mūlaphalaṣāḍavarogayogāṃ |
            <caesura/>
             seved rasāṃś ca vividhāṃ vividhaiḥ prakārair bhuñjīta vāpi laghurūkṣamanaḥsukhāni | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.57.12]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.13"><l>
            
            
             āsthāpanaṃ vividham atra virecanañ ca kuryān mṛdūni śirasaś ca virecanāni |
            <caesura/>
             trīṇy ūṣaṇāni triphalāṃ rajanīdvayañ ca cūrṇṇīkṛtāni yavaśūkavimiśritāni | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.57.13]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.14"><l>
            
            
             kṣaudrāyutāni vitaren mukhadhāvanārthaṃm anyāni tiktakaṭukāni ca bheṣajāni |
            <caesura/>
             mustādirājaphalavargadaśāṃgasiddhaḥ kvāthair jayen madhuyutair vividhaiś ca lehaiḥ || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.57.14]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.15"><l>
            
            
             mūtrāsavair guḍakṛtaiś ca tathāpy ariṣṭaiḥ kṣārāsavaiś ca madhumādhavatulyagandhaiḥ |
            <caesura/>
             syād eṣa eva kaphavātaharo vidhiś ca | śātiṃ gate hutabhuji praśamāya tasya | </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.57.15]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.16"><l>
            
            
             icchāvināśabhayajeṣu ca bādhakeṣu | bhāvād bhavāya vitaret khalu śalyatāpān |
            <caesura/>
             artheṣu cātipatiteṣu punarbhavāya paurāṇikaśrutimukhair avamānayeta iti || kāyaci || 20
            || 0 || </l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.57.16]</trailer>
</lg>

          <lg xml:id="SS.6.57.17"><l>
            
            
             cchardi hikkā śvāsakāsaḥ svaraghātam athāparaṃ |
            <caesura/>
             pratiśyāyaḥ krimiś caiva sodāvarttā visūcikā |
            <caesura/>
             arocakena ca tathā pūryate dvitīyo daśaḥ || ❈ ||</l>
<trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 6.57.17]</trailer>
</lg>

<standOff corresp="#SS.6.39.1" type="Note">
<note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.6.39.1-n1">In reading <foreign>-utpatti</foreign>,
                the scribe of H may have been influenced by the title <foreign>grahotpatti</foreign>
                of the immediately preceding adhyāya in his manuscript (he omits the
                  <foreign>yonivyāpat</foreign> adhyāya).</note>
</standOff>
<standOff corresp="#SS.6.39.3" type="Note">
<note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.6.39.3-n1">Dalhaṇa (p. 671) reported Jejjaṭa's
                reading <foreign>tridaśās tridaśeśvarāḥ</foreign>, which is a mixture of the
                readings of witnesses K and H.</note>
</standOff>
<standOff corresp="#SS.6.39.19cd" type="Emendation">
<note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.6.39.19cd-n1">Witnesses N and H read
                  <foreign>api-</foreign> for the emended reading <foreign>adhi-</foreign>. While
                both MSS seem to have read <foreign>pa</foreign>, it should be noted that in the
                script of MS K, <foreign>pa</foreign> and <foreign>dha</foreign> are somewhat
                similarly written. This suggests a reading <foreign>dha</foreign> that was misread
                early in the transmission.</note>
</standOff>
<standOff corresp="#SS.6.39.150" type="Testimonia">
<note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.6.39.150-n1">= <title>Carakasaṃhitā</title>
                6.3.188cd.</note>
</standOff>
<standOff corresp="#SS.6.39.164" type="Note">
<note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.6.39.164-n1">MS H breaks off at this point.</note>
</standOff>
